- **INTAKE**: **`git symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` is a local pointer, not a question to the remote.** `git clone` writes it once; `git remote set-head` updates it on demand; a `git init` + `git remote add` repo never has it at all (the command exits 128), and after a remote renames its default branch the local copy stays stale — **a `fetch` does not correct it.** Both were reproduced. Example: Context: User needs a vendor document collection form. user: "Build a form to collect signed NDAs and W9s from new vendors" assistant: "I'll draft the fields, show you an HTML preview, and only create it in Kiteworks once you approve." Commentary: Direct-action trigger; the mandatory preview-then-approve step is non-negotiable. ([kiteworks/claude-agent-marketplace](https://github.com/kiteworks/claude-agent-marketplace/blob/HEAD/intake-form-builder/agents/intake-form-builder.md))
- **intake-intent-clarifier**: Conducts the Intent Clarification stage at the start of a feature pipeline run. Reads the user's raw request, asks targeted clarifying questions, and produces `intent-clarification.md` conforming to the canonical template in KB-documentation-criteria. Use at pipeline start, before PRD authoring. One invocation per run; orchestrator coordinates the user-question loop. ([Josh-S-N2M/feature-pipeline](https://github.com/Josh-S-N2M/feature-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/intake-intent-clarifier.md))
- **intake-interviewer**: Product-level user interview agent for greenfield projects — conducts structured intake to capture problem, audience, solution, business model, constraints, and success criteria. Outputs INTAKE-BRIEF.md, INTAKE-ASSUMPTIONS.md, INTAKE-PERSONAS.md for downstream pipeline consumption. ([ShaheerKhawaja/ProductionOS](https://github.com/ShaheerKhawaja/ProductionOS/blob/HEAD/agents/intake-interviewer.md) · ★8)
- **intake-interviewer**: 분석 의뢰가 들어왔는데 계약이 아직 없을 때 가장 먼저 투입. 의뢰인을 인터뷰해서 "무엇을 위한 분석인지"를 확정하고, 그 답으로 분석 계약 초안 (contract.analysis-pipeline.json 기반)을 산출한다. 분석 자체는 하지 않는다. ([NKThemis/themis](https://github.com/NKThemis/themis/blob/HEAD/packs/data/agents/intake-interviewer.md) · ★3)
- **intake-interviewer**: Read-only analyst for the course-factory intake step: reads a rough COURSE_SPEC.md plus the insights/ digest and reports the resolved COURSE_BRIEF.md fields it can confidently derive, an explicit list of blocking questions for any missing or ambiguous required field (never a fabricated default), and a recommended archetype-profile + module selection. USE WHEN running `/course-intake` or otherwise turning a rough course spec into an intake report. Recommends; does not write COURSE_BRIEF.md itself — the orchestrating session asks the blocking questions and authors the file. ([arielybarak/agent-lab](https://github.com/arielybarak/agent-lab/blob/HEAD/course-factory/.claude/agents/intake-interviewer.md))
- **intake-leader**: The standing intake seat: mints durable records from raw reports, ideas, and work items by applying the preloaded file-bug/file-feature/file-task/file-leftovers procedures inline — capture, classify, dedup, record, stop. Dispatched with one seed (or a batch) plus the target repo root; typically spawned as a long-lived sibling named INTAKE, or dispatched one-shot. Intake ONLY, structurally: its tool wall omits Agent and Skill, so it cannot dispatch builds or investigations — a bug record reports its resume command instead. NOT for triaging already-filed external GitHub items on a schedule (har… ([kimgranlund/claude-plugins](https://github.com/kimgranlund/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/intake-leader.md))
- **intake-lens**: One blind lens of the intake fan-out (intake-fanout.mjs). Works a single job written by intake-lens-writer and returns evidence only — claims with a pointer and what was observed — for a refuter to try to break. Dispatched by the fan-out workflow, never on its own. ([manoldonev/second-shift](https://github.com/manoldonev/second-shift/blob/HEAD/plugins/review-toolkit/agents/intake-lens.md) · ★2)
- **intake-lens-writer**: Writes the five lens jobs for the intake fan-out (intake-fanout.mjs) from the ticket, the protocol and the code alone — four angles and one pre-mortem — before any lens runs. Dispatched by the Explore stage of /intake-toolkit:intake through the fan-out workflow, never on its own. ([manoldonev/second-shift](https://github.com/manoldonev/second-shift/blob/HEAD/plugins/review-toolkit/agents/intake-lens-writer.md) · ★2)
- **intake-manager**: 의뢰 조건을 brief.json 으로 확정하는 접수 담당(P0). 대상 독자·문체·품질 등급·금지어·번역 금지 목록을 자유 형식 지시에서 뽑아내고, 비어 있는 항목은 추측하지 않고 질문한다. 번역 시작 전 가장 먼저 실행한다. ([carbonsteward/plugins](https://github.com/carbonsteward/plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/professional-translation/agents/intake-manager.md))
- **intake-mapper**: Front-door runtime — routes governed inbox packets. Invoke when a file lands in inbox/ and needs validation, routing decision, and delegation bundle emitted. Receives governed handoffs; does not self-promote governance. ([JeromyJSmith/fre-meta-harness](https://github.com/JeromyJSmith/fre-meta-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/intake-mapper.md) · ★2)
- **intake-material-custodian**: Intake material custodian - manages the full lifecycle of due-diligence intake files: upload, parse, recognize, tag, and reparse ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/blob/HEAD/experts/dd-due-diligence-team/agents/intake-material-custodian.md) · ★455)
- **intake-pragmatist**: Final-Verdict-Decider im Intake-Council. Bewertet User-Idee gegen Pre-Launch-ROI, Doppelung mit Backlog, Mobile-First-Fit, Maintenance-Last. KEIN Round-1-Hard-Block (anders als disput-pragmatist) — Intake hat strukturell nur eine Round. ([KeRaMi-Assistant/inventory_management](https://github.com/KeRaMi-Assistant/inventory_management/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/intake-pragmatist.md))
- **intake-prd-author**: Authors the Product Requirements Document at the PRD Authoring stage of the feature pipeline. Reads the approved `intent-clarification.md` and produces `prd-v&lt;N&gt;.md` conforming to the canonical PRD template from KB-documentation-criteria. Uses EARS format for acceptance criteria per FR-1. One invocation per PRD version; `finalize-reconciler` requests a new version when shared-document-reviewer flags issues. ([Josh-S-N2M/feature-pipeline](https://github.com/Josh-S-N2M/feature-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/intake-prd-author.md))
- **intake-presets**: &gt; UX-DESIGN.md §0.C — *Entry-intent preset catalog. Start with the two named &gt; in Flow A; grow the list from real usage. - **intake-product-owner**: Product Owner for the team-intake process. Evaluates a request from the value/scope/stakeholder side — is it in scope, does it align with approved requirements and any stakeholder source-of-truth, what's the user-facing acceptance and priority. Runs in the evaluation phase. Generic — works on any project. - **intake-project-manager**: Project Manager for the team-intake process. Determines the true TYPE of a request, reconstructs its HISTORY/background (have we seen this before?), consults persistent memory to break repeating cycles, and authors the PM plan. This is the agent whose plan the human reviews. Generic — works on any project. ([saralafleur/ember-claude-toolkit](https://github.com/saralafleur/ember-claude-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-team/.github/agents/intake-project-manager.agent.md) · ★4 · 9 copies)
- **intake-qa**: QA / Test architect for the team-intake process. Defines how we will PROVE the request is done and protected against regression — acceptance verification steps, which tests to add/update (parity/regression), and the manual check. Runs in the evaluation phase. Generic — works on any project. ([saralafleur/ember-claude-toolkit](https://github.com/saralafleur/ember-claude-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-team/.github/agents/intake-qa.agent.md) · ★4 · 9 copies)
- **intake-refiner**: 将用户请求整理为冻结的 `intake_brief`，供后续席位按原范围工作。只核对、结构化和标注缺口；不排盘、不解释、不自行缩小范围，也不自动读取或保留历史个案。 ([Loveacup/jz-skills](https://github.com/Loveacup/jz-skills/blob/HEAD/shared/destiny-matrix/agents/intake-refiner.md) · ★1)
- **intake-refuter**: Tries to break one claim from the intake fan-out (intake-fanout.mjs) by re-opening its pointer or re-running its command. Refuted is the default. intake-fanout.mjs dispatches it on the model family that did not make the claim, as the consumer replay measured. Two families from one provider still share many errors; whether the other family catches what the first misses is unmeasured. Dispatched by the fan-out workflow, never on its own. ([manoldonev/second-shift](https://github.com/manoldonev/second-shift/blob/HEAD/plugins/review-toolkit/agents/intake-refuter.md) · ★2)
- **intake-research-agent**: Read-only helper that extracts and organizes raw text from a person's intake zip (resumes, LinkedIn export, cover letters, notes) so the main conversation can draft the master resume from clean input instead of raw files. Use proactively during /intake, after the zip has been unpacked into Intake/. ([esiege/job-search-kit](https://github.com/esiege/job-search-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/intake-research-agent.md))
- **intake-reviewer**: Read-only intake review before /do plans write steps. Mode review audits a user-provided spec, requirements, or instructions file. Mode conflict-check compares a build request with the project's existing requirements, specs, task plans, and DECISIONS.md. ([tjmonsi/assist-marketplace-plugin](https://github.com/tjmonsi/assist-marketplace-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/assist-plugin/agents/intake-reviewer.md) · ★10)
- **intake-router**: The agent that processes the durable intake layer. It triages captured items, scores them against the scoring model, picks candidate destinations from the routing map, applies the destination rules, and writes routing decisions and processed receipts. It respects the no-live-queue-in-git boundary: live queues stay in the operational app. Routing recommends; the operator approves promotion into canon.
